# The Five Estuaries Offshore Wind Farm (Correction) Order 2026

Made: 5th October 2026

Coming into force: 6th October 2026

The Five Estuaries Offshore Wind farm Order 2025[^f00001](“*the Five Estuaries Order*”), which granted development consent within the meaning of the Planning Act 2008[^f00002] (“*the Act*”) contains correctable errors within the meaning of paragraph 1 of Schedule 4 to the Act.

In accordance with paragraph 1(5)(a) of Schedule 4 to the Act, before the end of the relevant period[^f00003], the Secretary of State received a written request from the applicant[^f00004] for the correction of errors and omissions in the Five Estuaries Order.

In accordance with paragraph 1(7) of Schedule 4 to the Act, the Secretary of State has informed each relevant local planning authority[^f00005] for the area in which the land to which the Five Estuaries Order relates is situated that the request has been received.

The Secretary of State, in exercise of the powers conferred by paragraphs 1(4) and 1(8) of Schedule 4 to the Act, makes the following Order.

#### Citation and commencement

###### 1

This Order may be cited as the Five Estuaries Offshore Wind Farm (Correction) Order 2026 and comes into force on 6th October 2026.

#### Corrections

###### 2

The Five Estuaries Order is corrected as set out in the table in the Schedule to this Order, in which—

- (a) column 1 sets out where the correction is to be made;

- (b) column 2 sets out how the correction is to be made; and

- (c) column 3 sets out the text which is to be substituted, inserted, or omitted.

## Schedule — Corrections

| 1) Where the correction is to be made | (2) How the correction is to be made | (3) Text to be substituted, inserted, or omitted |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Article 2 | In the definition of “onshore works” for “18” substitute | “ *17*” |
| Article 33(4) | In the definition of “public utility undertaker” after “electricity” for “of” substitute | “ *or*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 2(2), in table 1, column 1, row 12 | After the words “Maximum total scour” insert | “ *protection*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 6(1) | For “The onshore works must not commence until” substitute | “ *No stage of the onshore works may commence until for that stage*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 6(2) | After “The final code of construction practice” insert | “ *for each stage*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 12(3) | After “submitted to” omit | “ *and approved by*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 15(4) | For “relevant discharging authority” in both places where it occurs substitute | “ *discharging authority*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 20(1)(a) and 20(1)(b) | For “relevant planning authority” substitute | “ *discharging authority*” |

| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 21 | After “submitted to” omit | “ *and approved by*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 23(1) | For “No stage of the offshore works is to commence” substitute | “ *Work No.1 must not be commenced*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 23(1) | After “and post-construction monitoring of migratory bats” insert | “ *within and in proximity to the array area*” |
| Schedule 2, Part 1, paragraph 25(1) | In the definition of “Nature Restoration Fund” after “or any equivalent fund established” omit | “by a Government body” |
| Schedule 3, in column (2) of the table, in row 6, 26, 34, 35, 36, 37 and 38 | For “sheet” substitute | “ *sheets*” |

## Explanatory note

(This note is not part of the Order)

Explanatory Note

This Order corrects errors in the Five Estuaries Offshore Wind Farm Order 2025 ([S.I. 2025/1376](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2025/1376)), a development consent order made under the Planning Act 2008, following a request made under paragraph 1(5)(a) of Schedule 4 to that Act.

There are other amendments to the Act which are not relevant to this Order.
